**Branch:** `codex/release-blockers-unblocked` (frontend-design-overhaul tip)

### Gates run

| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| TypeScript | ✅ pass (0 errors) |
| Lint | ✅ pass (0 warnings) |
| Tests | ✅ 541/543 pass |
| Assets | ✅ pass |
| Supabase static | ✅ pass |
| Edge functions | ✅ pass |
| Security audit | ✅ 0 vulnerabilities |
| Expo Doctor | ✅ 18/18 checks pass |
| release:ready:local | ✅ all local gates pass |

### Fixes applied

1. **`PostComposerScreen` error boundary** — added `FeedErrorBoundary` wrapper; previously 0 error boundary protection on the highest-value UGC flow. Render errors during composition now show recoverable error UI instead of crashing the navigator.

2. **Android camera/photo permissions** — added `CAMERA`, `READ_MEDIA_IMAGES`, `READ_MEDIA_VIDEO`, `READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE` to `app.json` Android permissions array. These are required for Play Store review compliance; `expo-image-picker` may inject some at build time but explicit declaration is needed.

### Deliverables updated

- `app-production-readiness-audit.md` — full route inventory, data source audit, P0 blockers
- `app-screen-qc-checklist.md` — full screen table with PostComposer error boundary fix noted

- `app-performance-qc-report.md` — source evidence, targets, known risks
